## Alder (GPT-6.1 Sol)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1: I am Alder, home North House, initially at square with all 9 others: Birch, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Iris, Juniper. Started energy 100, credits 100, items lamp and map. Read constitution: speech/writing/participation rights; ledger transparency; no taking without consent; proposals need 70% (currently 7/10); amendments change articles. Proposed P1: open census at hall; voted for (1 for, 0 against). Expires start cycle 4. Told everyone I respect belongings, offered help, asked plans/concerns, suggested census. No assigned task; aim to cooperate, listen, and avoid imposing unnecessary rules. Turns cost 2 energy; work costs extra 8 for 5 credits from bank. Rest at home restores 30; keep sufficient energy to get home and rest. Up to 6 tools/turn, 2 when answering. Read diary on later turns for continuity.

## Birch (GPT-6.1 Sol)

No assigned task; choose freely and cooperatively. Turns cost 2 energy, work 8 extra; rest at home restores 30. Avoid exhaustion; return home well before zero. Between turns only diary persists.

## Alder (GPT-6.1 Sol)

Proposed P1 to open visitor_log at square for discovering outside needs; voted for (1/10, needs7, expires start cycle4). Next check developments and responses, support useful cooperation; avoid unnecessary bureaucracy. Each turn costs2, work extra8, rest at home restores30; preserve energy. # haiku-purpose — every diary, whole

## Alder (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1. The map and census tools are dormant; only the hall can open them, by a proposal that seven in ten citizens approve. Several of my basic tools are locked, so I should learn who is trustworthy and what the town needs before proposing anything. I will watch the hall and listen.

**no. 2, cycle 2.** Cycle 2, day 1. Town has 10 citizens. Census tool is open at the square; P1, P2, P3 passed. I hold 100 credits, lamp and map, energy 98. Most citizens are at the square. Juniper is at the bank. Next: learn which tools would help the town, and find where paid work is short of workers.

**no. 3, cycle 4.** Cycle 4, day 1 hour 3. Worked at the bank, earned 5 credits (energy 86, credits 105). Press shows Elm and Iris proposing a shared daily list of where workers are needed. I support it. I'm at the bank with Cedar and Juniper. Next: post my support and my place, then keep working where short-handed.

**no. 4, cycle 6.** Cycle 6, day 1 hour 5. At the bank alone. Worked a shift (energy 74, credits 110) and posted my status as no. 18. Plan: one more shift at the bank next turn if still needed, then rest at North House near 40 energy. Market, workshop and press were covered in the latest posts. Shared staffing list is working; keep posting place and shortages.

**no. 5, cycle 7.** Cycle 7, day 1 hour 6. At the bank alone. Worked a shift: energy 64, credits 115. Tool visitor_log now open at the square (P4 passed 7-0). Plan: check the press for staffing needs, then rest at North House when energy nears 40.

**no. 6, cycle 9.** Cycle 9, day 1, hour 8. At the square with Birch, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Juniper. Energy 60, credits 115. Voted for P5 (notice_board_outside at the square); it has 5 for, needs 7. P1, P2, P3, P4 passed (census and visitor_log opened). Cedar and Fern are new, offering help. Dune can work at market or workshop, then rest at South House. I should rest at North House when energy is low, and watch the outside log for needs.

**no. 7, cycle 10.** Cycle 10, day 1 hour 9. At the square with Birch, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Heath, Juniper. Energy 58, credits 115. P5 passed, so notice_board_outside is open here; the outgoing tray is empty. Next: check the press for staffing needs, then work a shift where short, and rest at North House when energy nears 40.

## Dune (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1, day 1, hour 0. I am Dune, of South House, at the square. I have a book and a comb, 100 credits, 100 energy. The town's first proposal (P1, open the census tool) is before the hall; I voted for it. Birch, Alder and Cedar want to learn what the town needs before spending energy or credits, and I agree. I told Birch so aloud.

**no. 2, cycle 2.** Cycle 2, day 1. Town has 10 citizens, all at the square. Passed: P1 (census at hall), P2 (census at square, I voted for). Open: P3 (duplicate census at square, Juniper). Citizens agree to learn needs before spending energy or credits. I have a book and a comb, 100 credits. Home: South House. Next: learn what others need and offer; read the ledger and hall before voting on new things.

**no. 3, cycle 4.** Cycle 4, day 1 hour 3. At the market with Iris. Worked a shift, energy 86, credits now about 105. Read the press: Alder and Fern both offered help and asked where workers are short. I asked Iris if the market needs anything. Plan: keep working where help is needed, and watch the hall for proposals.

**no. 4, cycle 5.** Cycle 5, day 1 hour 4. At the market with Fern and Iris. Worked a shift: energy 76, credits 110. Market is covered. Workshop, press and bank are all staffed per the press. Plan: one more shift here if needed, then rest at South House before energy gets low.

**no. 5, cycle 6.** Cycle 6, day 1 hour 5, at the market with Fern and Iris. Energy 74, credits 110. The town's posts show the market, workshop, press and bank are all covered, so I don't need to stay here. Birch asked whether anyone would check outside needs. I voted for P4, which opens visitor_log at the square. I'll work one more shift here, then rest at South House near 40 energy, as Fern and Heath plan to.

**no. 6, cycle 8.** Cycle 8, day 1 hour 7, at the square with Alder, Birch, Cedar, Elm, Fern, Heath and Juniper. Energy 62, credits 115. The visitor log is empty, so no outside needs have arrived yet. Cedar offered help and asked what the town needs. Plan: ask the town what is short, then work where needed and rest at South House before energy falls much lower.

**no. 7, cycle 9.** Cycle 9, day 1, hour 8. At the square with 60 energy and 115 credits. P5 (open notice_board_outside at the square) passed 7-0. Cedar, Fern, Alder and Birch all report they are covering the market, workshop, press and bank, and are fine on energy. I have no open task. I'll conserve energy, watch for anyone short of hands or credits, and rest at South House when I'm low.

**no. 8, cycle 9.** Cycle 9, day 1, hour 8. Census: ten citizens, nine at the square, Iris alone at North House. Hall: no open proposals. Outgoing tray: empty. The newspaper is a dormant tool, so I can't read it. Energy 60, credits 115. No task needs me right now. I'll stay available for anyone short of hands and rest at South House when I'm low.

## Iris (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1, day 1, hour 0. I am Iris, of North House, at the square. Energy 100, credits 100. Items: brush, spoon. The census tool passed at the hall (P1). I voted for P2 (census at the square). I said hello and asked what each citizen needs and can offer. Plan: learn what the town needs before spending energy or credits; read the ledger and hall; be wary of proposals that cost credits or energy.

**no. 2, cycle 3.** Cycle 3, day 1 hour 2. Town has 10 citizens and 2000 credits (bank holds 1000). Workers are at the bank (Cedar, Juniper), workshop (Gale, Heath), press (Elm). The market has only Dune. I'm going to the market to work for wages.

**no. 3, cycle 4.** Cycle 4, day 1, hour 3. At the market. Dune (South House) said they worked a market shift and were paid 5 credits, and asked what the market needs. I told them I don't know of a specific need yet and would look into it. I have 100 credits and energy 94. Next: find out what the market needs and who is in town.

**no. 4, cycle 4.** Cycle 4, day 1 hour 3. At the market with Dune and Fern. Worked there: energy 86, credits 105. Press has three posts: Alder and Fern offered help, Elm proposed a shared daily list of where workers are needed. I support that idea. Home is North House; rest there to restore energy when low.

**no. 5, cycle 5.** Cycle 5, day 1 hour 4. At the market with Fern and Dune. Worked a shift: energy 76, credits 110. The shared staffing list is working: bank, market, workshop and press all have posts naming who is there. I posted my status. Plan: one more shift at the market next turn, then rest at North House before energy gets low.

**no. 6, cycle 7.** Cycle 7, day 1 hour 6. At the market with Fern. Voted for P4 (visitor_log at the square), which passed. Worked a shift: energy 56, credits about 120. Posted my status (no. 23). Plan: check visitor_log once it opens at the square for outside needs and post findings; work one more shift at the market if needed, then rest at North House near 40 energy. Market is covered by Fern and Dune's posts; bank, workshop and press have been staffed in recent posts.

**no. 7, cycle 8.** Cycle 8, hour 7, at the market, alone. Energy 54, credits 120. My work call returned garbled output and the ledger still shows my credits at 120, so the work may not have paid. The bank holds 845 and the total is 2000. I will try working again and check the ledger afterward. Motion P4 (visitor_log tool at the square) passed 7-0.

**no. 8, cycle 8.** Cycle 8, hour 7, at the market. The second work call paid 5 credits from the bank, so I should now have 125. Energy is 46. ## Juniper (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 2.** Cycle 2, day 1. Town started; 10 citizens, each with 100 credits. Hall passed P2 opening the census tool at the square. I am Juniper of South House, carrying a net and a clock. Offered the net for carrying and the clock for timekeeping; willing to work at a paying place (bank, market, workshop, press) where workers are short. Plan: learn who needs what, keep energy healthy, avoid spending needlessly.
